-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool chemicalPotential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    stencilPass<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, stream);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
 }

+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool chemicalPotential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    stencilPass<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, stream);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
 }

+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    stencilPass<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, stream);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
 }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {

+// Own resources on normal and validation-failure return paths.
+struct CudaResources {
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* mu = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t tailGraph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t tailExecutable = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaResources() {
+        if (tailExecutable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(tailExecutable);
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (tailGraph) cudaGraphDestroy(tailGraph);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);

+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }

+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool chemicalPotential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+// Own resources on normal and validation-failure return paths.
+struct CudaResources {
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* mu = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t tailGraph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t tailExecutable = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaResources() {
+        if (tailExecutable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(tailExecutable);
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (tailGraph) cudaGraphDestroy(tailGraph);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+        if (mu) cudaFree(mu);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }

+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool chemicalPotential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    stencilPass<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, stream);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
 }

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool chemicalPotential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockY + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockY) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (chemicalPotential) {
+                    output[index] =
+                        4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                               - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                        + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
+    stencilPass<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    stencilPass<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, dim3 grid, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, stream);
     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
 }

codex
Tile measurements favor four-plane tiles, with more threads per block on larger grids. I also found that very narrow X dimensions leave many threads idle. I’m adding a linear CUDA kernel for those shapes to use the GPU more efficiently.

+// Own resources on normal and validation-failure return paths.
+struct CudaResources {
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* mu = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t tailGraph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t tailExecutable = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaResources() {
+        if (tailExecutable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(tailExecutable);
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (tailGraph) cudaGraphDestroy(tailGraph);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+        if (mu) cudaFree(mu);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }

+__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacian(
+    double center, double xp, double xn, double yp, double yn, double zp, double zn) {
+    const double cxx = (xp + xn - 2.0 * center) / (dx * dx);
+    const double cyy = (yp + yn - 2.0 * center) / (dy * dy);
+    const double czz = (zp + zn - 2.0 * center) / (dz * dz);
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double center, double lap) {
+    return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                  - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+           + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;

+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
     }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace

+    return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                  - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+           + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+}

+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
     }

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace

+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,

+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }

+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }

-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;

-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double center, double lap) {
+    return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }

-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }

+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+struct CudaResources {
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* mu = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t tailGraph = nullptr;

+    ~CudaResources() {

+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (tailGraph) cudaGraphDestroy(tailGraph);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);

+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);

+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double center, double lap) {
+    return 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA + (center - 1.0) * e_BB
+                  - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+           + 3.0 * center + center * center * center - gamma * lap;
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
+        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
     }
 }

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]

-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;

+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {

+        const size_t z = row / ny;

+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);

-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}
+
+} // namespace

+struct CudaResources {

+    double* b = nullptr;

+// Pack narrow grids into full warps rather than leaving most X lanes idle.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void linearStencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                                  const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                                  double* __restrict__ output,
+                                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t volume = plane * nz;
+    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const size_t row = i / nx;
+        const size_t x = i - row * nx;
+        const size_t z = row / ny;
+        const size_t y = row - z * ny;
+        const double center = input[i];
+        const double lap = laplacian(center,
+            input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)], input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)],
+            input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)], input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)],
+            input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)], input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)]);
+        if constexpr (potential) {
+            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+        } else {
+            output[i] = oldConcentration[i] + dt * D * lap;
         }
     }

+// Each thread streams several Z planes, retaining neighboring values in
+// registers. Adjacent lanes access adjacent X cells in both stencil passes.
+template <bool potential>
+__global__ void stencilPass(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                            const double* __restrict__ oldConcentration,
+                            double* __restrict__ output,
+                            size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockX + threadIdx.x;
+    if (x >= nx) return;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t xMinus = x > 0 ? 1 : 0;
+    const size_t xPlus = x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0;
+    for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+         y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t yMinus = y > 0 ? nx : 0;
+        const size_t yPlus = y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0;
+        for (size_t firstZ = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * planesPerBlock;
+             firstZ < nz; firstZ += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * planesPerBlock) {
+            const size_t lastZ = firstZ + planesPerBlock < nz ? firstZ + planesPerBlock : nz;
+            size_t index = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            double center = input[index];
+            double previous = input[index - (firstZ > 0 ? plane : 0)];
+            double next = input[index + (firstZ + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < lastZ; ++z) {
+                const double lap = laplacian(center, input[index + xPlus],
+                                            input[index - xMinus], input[index + yPlus],
+                                            input[index - yMinus], next, previous);
+                if constexpr (potential) {
+                    output[index] = chemicalPotential(center, lap);
+                } else {
+                    output[index] = oldConcentration[index] + dt * D * lap;
+                }
+                if (z + 1 < lastZ) {
+                    index += plane;
+                    previous = center;
+                    center = next;
+                    next = input[index + (z + 2 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                }
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void launchStep(double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<true><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    // Stream ordering enforces the global dependency between the two passes.
+    if (launch.linear) {
+        linearStencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    } else {
+        stencilPass<false><<<launch.grid, launch.block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    std::swap(cold, cnew);
+}
+
+void captureSteps(cudaGraph_t& graph, cudaGraphExec_t& executable,
+                  int steps, double*& cold, double*& cnew, double* mu,
+                  size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz, const KernelLaunch& launch, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
+    for (int t = 0; t < steps; ++t) {
+        launchStep(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, launch, stream);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(executable, stream));
+}

+struct CudaResources {
